## 終端配置屬性 · 代碼示例

**id**　5464f122b27e21f651-10

**english**　Terminal Configuration properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 公共設施網絡屬性
- 終端配置

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/terminal-configurations.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/terminal-configurations.html

**text**　'''****************************************************************************
Name:        DescribeUtilityNetworkProperties.py
Description: This script reports the properties of a utility network
Created by:  Esri
****************************************************************************'''

# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Describe function on a Utility Network
UN = "C:\\MyProject\\databaseConn.sde\\mygdb.USER1.Naperville\\mygdb.USER1.ElectricNetwork"
d = arcpy.Describe(UN)

# Terminal Configuration properties
termconfigs = d.terminalConfigurations
for tc in termconfigs:
    print("*** - Terminal Configuration Properties - ***")
    print(f"ID: {tc.terminalConfigurationId}")
    print(f"Name: {tc.terminalConfigurationName}")
    print(f"Traversability Model: {tc.traversabilityModel}")
    print(f"Default Configuration: {tc.defaultConfiguration} \n")

    # For each terminal in the terminals object:
    for t in tc.terminals:
        print(" -- Terminal Properties -- ")
        print(f"Terminal ID: {t.terminalId}")
        print(f"Terminal Name: {t.terminalName}")
        print(f"Terminal Is Upstream: {t.isUpstreamTerminal} \n")

    # For each configuration in the valid configuration paths object:
    try:
        for lc in tc.validConfigurationPaths:
            print(" - Configuration Properties - ")
            print(f"Configuration Id: {lc.id}")
            print(f"Configuration Name: {lc.name}")
            print(f"Description: {lc.description} \n")
            try:
                for tp in lc.terminalPaths:
                    print(f"From terminal id: {tp.fromTerminalId}")
                    print(f"To terminal id: {tp.toTerminalId}")
            except:
                print(f"{lc.name} does not have any terminal paths \n")
    except:
        print(f"{t.terminalName} does not have any valid configuration paths \n")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/terminal-configurations.html#code-sample

## SetSeverityLevel · 代碼示例

**id**　54e7492b1010a280e5-6

**english**　SetSeverityLevel

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 消息和錯誤處理

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/setseveritylevel.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/setseveritylevel.html

**text**　import arcpy

fc1 = 'c:/resources/resources.gdb/boundary'
fc2 = 'c:/resources/resources.gdb/boundary2'

# Set the severity level to 1 (tool warnings will throw an exception)
arcpy.SetSeverityLevel(1)
print("Severity is set to : {0}".format(arcpy.GetSeverityLevel()))

try:
    # FeatureCompare returns warning messages when a miscompare is
    # found.  This normally would not cause an exception; however, by
    # setting the severity level to 1, all tool warnings will also
    # return an exception.
    arcpy.management.FeatureCompare(fc1, fc2, "OBJECTID")
except arcpy.ExecuteWarning:
    print(arcpy.GetMessages(1))
except arcpy.ExecuteError:
    print(arcpy.GetMessages(2))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/setseveritylevel.html#code-sample

- field_names[field_names,...]
- A list of field names of the output type whose values will be returned by the cursor. You can get the names of the fields supported by an output type using the fieldNames method. Learn more about the fields included in each output type In addition to regular fields, you can also access the geometry of the output using one of the following geometry tokens: SHAPE@XY—A tuple of the feature's centroid's x- and y-coordinates. SHAPE@XYZ—A tuple of the feature's centroid's x-, y-, and z-coordinates. SHAPE@JSON— The Esri JSON string representing the geometry. SHAPE@WKB—The well-known binary (WKB) representation for OGC geometry. It provides a portable representation of a geometry value as a contiguous stream of bytes. SHAPE@WKT—The well-known text (WKT) representation for OGC geometry. It provides a portable representation of a geometry value as a text string. SHAPE@—A geometry object for the feature. The SHAPE@XY and SHAPE@XYZ tokens are only supported for point-based output types. The x-, y-, and z-values returned when using the SHAPE@XY and SHAPE@XYZ tokens are in the spatial reference reported by the solver result object's spatialReference property, and this is typically the same as the spatial reference of the network data source used for the analysis. 提示： Retrieving geometry for the Lines output type can be slow. For best performance, do not request geometry using any of the geometry tokens.
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/network-analyst/origindestinationcostmatrixresult.html#method-searchCursor

## count · 注:

**id**　54f6636d6a8d468fda-35

**english**　OriginDestinationCostMatrixResult

**chapter**　
- Python
- Network Analyst 模塊
- 類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/origindestinationcostmatrixresult.html#method-toArrowTable

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/origindestinationcostmatrixresult.html

**parameter**　max_batch_size

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- max_batch_size （可選）
- The maximum number of records to include in a single record batch in the output Arrow table. When no value or None is specified, a value of 2,147,483,647 is used. If the total number of records is smaller than the specified max_batch_size, the output Arrow table and file will contain only one record batch. For very large analyses, writing the output in batches prevents out-of-memory errors. The method always returns a single Arrow table and output file (if the file_name parameter is specified), but the presence of multiple record batches in the output may influence how you handle it in subsequent code. When an Arrow table or file containing multiple record batches is converted to another format, such as a Pandas DataFrame, you may not be able to do a zero-copy read of the entire table or file. In a zero-copy read, the data can be accessed without additional serialization overhead to load it in RAM. To achieve this zero-copy read behavior, your code may need to handle one record batch at a time. For the Lines output type, for most applications, it is important to keep all records associated with the same origin together in the same batch. This ensures that you can do a zero-copy read of all records for that origin for post-processing or further analysis. If the number of records associated with a given origin exceeds the specified max_batch_size, the method will override the max_batch_size value, and the resulting record batches will increase to the minimum size needed to store all records associated with the origin. The value for max_batch_size cannot exceed 2,147,483,647.
- Integer

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/network-analyst/origindestinationcostmatrixresult.html#method-toArrowTable

## SearchNeighborhoodSmoothCircular · 代碼示例

**id**　5560793102b96fd0f7-11

**english**　SearchNeighborhoodSmoothCircular

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 地統計分析

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/searchneighborhoodsmoothcircular-class-.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/searchneighborhoodsmoothcircular-class-.html

**text**　# Name: EmpiricalBayesianKriging_Example_02.py
# Description: Bayesian kriging approach whereby many models created around the
#   semivariogram model estimated by the restricted maximum likelihood algorithm is used.
# Requirements: Geostatistical Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/gapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inPointFeatures = "ca_ozone_pts.shp"
zField = "ozone"
outLayer = "outEBK"
outRaster = "C:/gapyexamples/output/ebkout"
cellSize = 10000.0
transformation = "NONE"
maxLocalPoints = 50
overlapFactor = 0.5
numberSemivariograms = 100
# Set variables for search neighborhood
radius = 300000
smooth = 0.6
searchNeighbourhood = arcpy.SearchNeighborhoodSmoothCircular(radius, smooth)
outputType = "PREDICTION"
quantileValue = ""
thresholdType = ""
probabilityThreshold = ""

# Run EmpiricalBayesianKriging
arcpy.ga.EmpiricalBayesianKriging(inPointFeatures, zField, outLayer, outRaster,
                                  cellSize, transformation, maxLocalPoints, overlapFactor, numberSemivariograms,
                                  searchNeighbourhood, outputType, quantileValue, thresholdType, probabilityThreshold)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/searchneighborhoodsmoothcircular-class-.html#properties

**id**　55d7f807136682b365-1

**english**　& (Boolean And) operator

- 影像分析模塊
- 運算符
- 布爾運算符

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/boolean-and-operator.html

**text**　對兩個輸入網格的像元值執行“布爾與”運算。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/boolean-and-operator.html#summary

## & (Boolean And) operator · 討論

**id**　55d7f807136682b365-2

**english**　& (Boolean And) operator

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 運算符
- 布爾運算符

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/boolean-and-operator.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/boolean-and-operator.html

**text**　一個或多個輸入（操作數）是網格時， & 運算符將執行“布爾與”運算。 如果兩個輸入（操作數）都為數值，則 & 運算符將執行“按位與”運算。 有關如何使用運算符的詳細信息，請參閱 使用運算符 。
當表達式中存在多個運算符時，運算符不一定按照從左到右的順序運行。 具有最高優先級值的運算符將首先運行。 有關詳細信息，請參閱使用 “地圖代數”中的運算符 中的運算符優先級表。 可使用括號來控制運行順序。
布爾型運算符（ ~ 、 & 、 ^ 、 | ）的優先級比關系運算符（ < 、 <= 、 > 、 >= 、 == 、 != ）的優先級高。 當布爾型運算符在相同表達式中用作關系運算符時，將先運行布爾型運算符。 要更改運算符的運行順序，請使用括號。
當在單個表達式中連續使用多個關系或布爾運算符時，在某些情況下，該表達式可能會運行失敗。 要避免此問題，請在表達式中使用適當的括號，以便明確地定義運算符的運行順序。 有關詳細信息，請參閱 復雜語句規則 。
要進行該布爾運算，需要兩個輸入值。
使用此運算符時輸入的順序并不重要。
如果輸入值為浮點型，則值會在執行布爾運算前通過截斷轉換為整型值。 輸出值始終是整數。
執行“布爾與”運算的另一個方法為 a &= b ，其為 a = a & b 的另一種寫法。
如果兩個輸入都是單波段網格，或者輸入之一是常量，則輸出將是單波段網格。
如果兩個輸入都是多波段網格，則運算符將從一個輸入對每個波段執行運算，而輸出將為多波段網格。 每個多波段輸入中的波段數必須相同。
如果輸入之一是多波段網格，而另一個輸入是常量，則該運算符將使用多波段輸入中每個波段的常量值執行預算，而輸出將為多波段網格。
如果兩個輸入均為具有相同數量變量的 多維網格 ，則該運算符將對具有相同維度值的所有剖切片執行運算，并且輸出將為多維網格。 輸入中的變量必須具有相同維度或常見維度，但不能具有不常見的維度。
如果兩個輸入均具有一個變量但名稱不同，請將 匹配多維變量 環境設置為 False 以執行該運算。
如果輸入中的一個是多維網格，而另一個輸入是常量，則運算符將使用常量值對所有變量的所有切片執行運算，并且輸出將為多維網格。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/boolean-and-operator.html#discussion

**text**　# Name: Op_BooleanAnd_Ex_02.py
# Description: Performs a Boolean And operation on the cell values
#              of two input rasters
# Requirements: Image Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/iapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster1 = Raster("degs")
inRaster2 = Raster("negs")

# Run BooleanAnd
outBooleanAnd = inRaster1 & inRaster2

# Save the output
outBooleanAnd.save("C:/iapyexamples/output/outbooland")

**text**　Xarray 是一個 Python 模塊，支持帶有維度、座標和屬性標注的多維數組。它基于 NumPy 和 pandas 構建并與之整合，可處理 Python SciPy 生態系統中的多維資料以用于數值計算。有關詳細信息，請參閱 xarray 文檔 。
使用 Python 處理 xarray.DataArray 后，您可能需要將其轉換為 ArcGIS 多維網格，以便在 ArcGIS 環境中使用。

**content_version**　3.7

## XarrayToRaster · 代碼示例

**id**　55ddebbb85aa136792-7

**english**　XarrayToRaster

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 轉換

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/xarraytoraster.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/xarraytoraster.html

**text**　import arcpy
import xarray

# Get input Raster multidimensional information
inRas = arcpy.Raster('C:/data/temperature_3hour.crf', True)
inRas.mdinfo

# Convert Raster to Xarray Dataset
xrr = arcpy.ia.RasterToXarray(inRas)

# Upsample the data from originally 3 hour to hourly time interval
newXrr = xrr.resample(StdTime='1H').nearest(tolerance="1H")

#Convert Xarray dataset back to raster
newRaster = arcpy.ia.XarrayToRaster(newXrr)
newRaster.save("C:/output/temperature_hourly.crf")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/xarraytoraster.html#code-sample

## Geometry · 討論

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-2

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　在許多地理處理工作流中，您可能需要使用座標和幾何信息運行特定操作，但不一定想經歷創建新（臨時）圖徵類、使用光標填充圖徵類、使用圖徵類，然后刪除臨時圖徵類的過程。 可以使用幾何對象替代輸入和輸出，從而使地理處理變得更簡單。 使用 Geometry 、 Multipoint 、 PointGeometry 、 Polygon 或 Polyline 類可以從頭開始創建幾何對象。
在創建幾何對象的過程中，將執行一個簡化過程，以使幾何根據其幾何類型在拓撲上保持一致。 例如，將校正可能自相交的面，或包含錯誤環方向的面。 座標可能會在幾何空間參考的分辨率范圍內移動。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#discussion
